function normalizeHeaderText(value) {
  return String(value ?? "")
    .replace(/\r?\n/g, " ")
    .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
    .trim();
}

function detectHeaderRowIndex(rows) {
  const source = Array.isArray(rows) ? rows : [];
  const scanLimit = Math.min(source.length, 12);
  let bestIndex = -1;
  let bestScore = -Infinity;

  for (let index = 0; index < scanLimit; index += 1) {
    const cells = (source[index] || []).map(normalizeHeaderText);
    const nonEmpty = cells.filter(Boolean);
    if (nonEmpty.length < 2) continue;

    const uniqueCount = new Set(nonEmpty).size;
    const duplicatePenalty = nonEmpty.length - uniqueCount;
    const knownHeaderHits = nonEmpty.filter((value) =>
      /^(학생ID|학번|성명|사용성명|학년|반|번호|일자|운영일|일정ID|프로그램ID|강좌ID|기록ID|상태|구분|설정키|파일구분|스프레드시트ID|탭명|탭용도)$/.test(value),
    ).length;
    const titlePenalty = nonEmpty.length <= 2 && /UEP|표준|시트|DB|관리|기준/.test(nonEmpty.join(" ")) ? 8 : 0;
    const score = nonEmpty.length * 3 + uniqueCount + knownHeaderHits * 8 - duplicatePenalty * 2 - titlePenalty;

    if (score > bestScore) {
      bestScore = score;
      bestIndex = index;
    }
  }

  return bestIndex;
}

function headerLookupKey(value) {
  return normalizeHeaderText(value).replace(/[\s·_\-()\[\]{}]/g, "

async function fetchLiveData({ force = false, credentials = null } = {}) {
  if (!force && liveDataCache && Date.now() - liveDataFetchedAt < 120000) return liveDataCache;
  // 0.81.40: 관리자 로컬 서비스계정과 담임 School Read API 세션을 동일 읽기 파이프라인으로 연결합니다.
  const auth = await getReadonlySheetsAuth(credentials);
  const token = auth.token;
  const entries = [
    ...Object.entries(SHEET_RANGES),
    ["52_대입기초", "'52_대입기초'!A1:N500"],
    ["53_전형이해", "'53_전형이해'!A1:N500"],
    ["53A_전형세부유형DB", "'53A_전형세부유형DB'!A1:R500"],
    ["53B_전형유형별대학DB", "'53B_전형유형별대학DB'!A1:P3000"],
    ["56_대학입시마스터", "'56_대학입시마스터'!A1:R500"],
    ["56A_대학상담포인트DB", "'56A_대학상담포인트DB'!A1:P1200"],
    ["57_내신산정DB", "'57_내신산정DB'!A1:Y1200"],
    ["58_권장과목DB", "'58_권장과목DB'!A1:P2000"],
    ["18_학교교육과정DB", "'18_학교교육과정DB'!A1:N1000"],
    ["54_수능최저DB", "'54_수능최저DB'!A1:U2000"],
    ["55_대학입결DB", "'55_대학입결DB'!A1:K5000"],
  ];
  const matrices = {};
  // 기본정보 연결시트를 가장 먼저, 작은 묶음으로 읽습니다.
  // 탭 추가로 URL이 길어지거나 특정 탭이 잠시 실패해도 전체 연결이 끊기지 않도록 합니다.
  const chunkSize = 12;
  const chunks = [];
  for (let offset = 0; offset < entries.length; offset += chunkSize) {
    chunks.push({ offset, chunk: entries.slice(offset, offset + chunkSize) });
  }
  await mapWithConcurrency(chunks, 1, async ({ offset, chunk }) => {
    try {
      const valueRanges = await readSheetBatch(token, UEP_SPREADSHEET_ID, chunk.map(([, range]) => range));
      assignValueRangesByRange(matrices,chunk,valueRanges);
    } catch (chunkError) {
      console.warn(`[UEP] 기본정보 묶음 조회 실패(${offset + 1}~${offset + chunk.length}), 개별 재시도`, chunkError?.message || chunkError);
      await mapWithConcurrency(chunk, 1, async ([logicalName, range]) => {
        try {
          const one = await readSheetBatch(token, UEP_SPREADSHEET_ID, [range]);
          matrices[logicalName] = one?.[0]?.values || [];
        } catch (sheetError) {
          matrices[logicalName] = [];
          console.warn(`[UEP] 기본정보 탭 조회 실패: ${logicalName}`, sheetError?.message || sheetError);
        }
      });
    }
  });
  entries.forEach(([name]) => { if (!matrices[name]) matrices[name] = []; });

  // 선택보고서 정규화 탭의 구조가 손상된 경우 구글폼 원본으로 복구한 뒤 읽습니다.
  // 읽기 전용 담임 세션에서는 정규화 탭 자동복구(쓰기)를 실행하지 않습니다.
  if(auth.mode!=='school_read_api') await ensureSelectedReportNormalization(token);

  // 학생응답 가공 결과: 현재 실제 존재하는 데이터 처리 탭만 직접 읽습니다.
  // 선택과목은 응답수집 06_선택과목응답을 별도 읽고 기존 fallback 로직으로 구성합니다.
  const processingRanges = [
    ["02_학사외출_일자별", "'02_학사외출_일자별'!A1:AD10000"],
    ["20_공통보고서_정규화", "'20_공통보고서_정규화'!A1:X5000"],
    ["21_선택보고서_정규화", "'21_선택보고서_정규화'!A1:X5000"

  return {ok:true,operationalSyncedAt,syncedAt:operationalSyncedAt,patch};
}

async function fetchLiveData({ force = false, credentials = null } = {}) {
  if (!force && liveDataCache && Date.now() - liveDataFetchedAt < 120000) return liveDataCache;
  // 0.81.40: 관리자 로컬 서비스계정과 담임 School Read API 세션을 동일 읽기 파이프라인으로 연결합니다.
  const auth = await getReadonlySheetsAuth(credentials);
  const token = auth.token;
  const entries = [
    ...Object.entries(SHEET_RANGES),
    ["52_대입기초", "'52_대입기초'!A1:N500"],
    ["53_전형이해", "'53_전형이해'!A1:N500"],
    ["53A_전형세부유형DB", "'53A_전형세부유형DB'!A1:R500"],
    ["53B_전형유형별대학DB", "'53B_전형유형별대학DB'!A1:P3000"],
    ["56_대학입시마스터", "'56_대학입시마스터'!A1:R500"],
    ["56A_대학상담포인트DB", "'56A_대학상담포인트DB'!A1:P1200"],
    ["57_내신산정DB", "'57_내신산정DB'!A1:Y1200"],
    ["58_권장과목DB", "'58_권장과목DB'!A1:P2000"],
    ["18_학교교육과정DB", "'18_학교교육과정DB'!A1:N1000"],
    ["54_수능최저DB", "'54_수능최저DB'!A1:U2000"],
    ["55_대학입결DB", "'55_대학입결DB'!A1:K5000"],
  ];
  const matrices = {};
  // 기본정보 연결시트를 가장 먼저, 작은 묶음으로 읽습니다.
  // 탭 추가로 URL이 길어지거나 특정 탭이 잠시 실패해도 전체 연결이 끊기지 않도록 합니다.
  const chunkSize = 12;
  const chunks = [];
  for (let offset = 0; offset < entries.length; offset += chunkSize) {
    chunks.push({ offset, chunk: entries.slice(offset, offset + chunkSize) });
  }
  await mapWithConcurrency(chunks, 1, async ({ offset, chunk }) => {
    try {
      const valueRanges = await readSheetBatch(token, UEP_SPREADSHEET_ID, chunk.map(([, range]) => range));
      assignValueRangesByRange(matrices,chunk,valueRanges);
    } catch (chunkError) {
      console.warn(`[UEP] 기본정보 묶음 조회 실패(${offset + 1}~${offset + chunk.length}), 개별 재시도`, chunkError?.message || chunkError);
      await mapWithConcurrency(chunk, 1, async ([logicalName, range]) => {
        try {
          const one = await readSheetBatch(token, UEP_SPREADSHEET_ID, [range]);
          matrices[logicalName] = one?.[0]?.values || [];
        } catch (sheetError) {
          matrices[logicalName] = [];
          console.warn(`[UEP] 기본정보 탭 조회 실패: ${logicalName}`, sheetError?.message || sheetError);
        }
      });
    }
  });
  entries.forEach(([name]) => { if (!matrices[name]) matrices[name] = []; });

  // 선택보고서 정규화 탭의 구조가 손상된 경우 구글폼 원본으로 복구한 뒤 읽습니다.
  // 읽기 전용 담임 세션에서는 정규화 탭 자동복구(쓰기)를 실행하지 않습니다.
  if(auth.mode!=='school_read_api') await ensureSelectedReportNormalization(token);

  // 학생응답 가공 결과: 현재 실제 존재하는 데이터 처리 탭만 직접 읽습니다.

function classifyCalendarStatus(text) {
  const value=String(text||"");
  if(/취소|폐지/.test(value)) return "취소";
  if(/확정/.test(value)) return "확정";
  if(/예약|대관|사용/.test(value)) return "예약";
  if(/예정|계획|공고|제출기간/.test(value)) return "예정";
  return "상태 미기재";
}
function parseOwner(text) {
  const m=String(text||"").match(/^\(([^)]+)\)/);
  const token=(m?.[1]||"").trim();
  if(!token) return ["",""];
  if(/부$|부\s/.test(token)) return [token.replace(/\s*확정$/,""),""];
  const parts=token.split(/\s+/);
  if(parts.length>1 && /부$/.test(parts[0])) return [parts[0],parts.slice(1).join(" ")];
  return ["",token.replace(/\s*확정$/," ").trim()];
}

  return response.status===204?{}:response.json();
}

function googleUserOAuthRecoveryKey(salt){
  const identity=`${os.hostname()}|${os.userInfo().username}|UEP-GOOGLE-USER-OAUTH-v1`;
  return crypto.scryptSync(identity,salt,32);
}
function encodeGoogleUserOAuthRecovery(value){
  const salt=crypto.randomBytes(16),iv=crypto.randomBytes(12),key=googleUserOAuthRecoveryKey(salt);
  const cipher=crypto.createCipheriv('aes-256-gcm',key,iv);
  const encrypted=Buffer.concat([cipher.update(JSON.stringify(value),'utf8'),cipher.final()]);
  return {version:1,algorithm:'aes-256-gcm',salt:salt.toString('base64'),iv:iv.toString('base64'),tag:cipher.getAuthTag().toString('base64'),data:encrypted.toString('base64')};
}
function decodeGoogleUserOAuthRecovery(payload){
  if(!payload||payload.version!==1||payload.algorithm!=='aes-256-gcm')throw new Error('invalid OAuth recovery token');
  const salt=Buffer.from(payload.salt,'base64'),iv=Buffer.from(payload.iv,'base64'),tag=Buffer.from(payload.tag,'base64'),data=Buffer.from(payload.data,'base64');
  const decipher=crypto.createDecipheriv('aes-256-gcm',googleUserOAuthRecoveryKey(salt),iv);decipher.setAuthTag(tag);

async function readGoogleUserOAuth(){
  for(const target of [googleUserOAuthCanonicalPath(),googleUserOAuthPath()]){
    try{
      const encrypted=await fs.readFile(target);
      if(safeStorage.isEncryptionAvailable()){
        try{const value=JSON.parse(safeStorage.decryptString(encrypted));if(value&&typeof value==='object'){await saveGoogleUserOAuth(value);return value;}}catch{}
      }
      try{const legacy=JSON.parse(encrypted.toString('utf8'));if(legacy&&typeof legacy==='object'){await saveGoogleUserOAuth(legacy);return legacy;}}catch{}
    }catch{}
  }
  try{
    const recovered=await readGoogleUserOAuthRecovery();
    if(recovered&&typeof recovered==='object'){await saveGoogleUserOAuth(recovered);return recovered;}
  }catch{}
  return null;
}

function validateServiceAccount(value) {
  return Boolean(
    value && value.type === "service_account" &&
    typeof value.client_email === "string" &&
    typeof value.private_key === "string" &&
    value.private_key.startsWith("-----BEGIN PRIVATE KEY-----") &&
    value.token_uri === "https://oauth2.googleapis.com/token"
  );
}

async function disconnectGoogleUser(){
  for(const file of [googleUserOAuthCanonicalPath(),googleUserOAuthCanonicalRecoveryPath(),googleUserOAuthPath(),googleUserOAuthRecoveryPath()]){

async function getSheetsToken(credentials) {
  const now = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);
  const header = base64url(JSON.stringify({ alg: "RS256", typ: "JWT" }));
  const claims = base64url(JSON.stringify({
    iss: credentials.client_email,
    scope: "https://www.googleapis.com/auth/spreadsheets",
    aud: credentials.token_uri,
    iat: now,
    exp: now + 3600,
  }));
  const unsigned = `${header}.${claims}`;
  const signature = crypto.sign("RSA-SHA256", Buffer.from(unsigned), credentials.private_key).toString("base64url");
  const body = new URLSearchParams({
    grant_type: "urn:ietf:params:oauth:grant-type:jwt-bearer",
    assertion: `${unsigned}.${signature}`,
  });
  const response = await fetch(credentials.token_uri, {
    method: "POST",
    headers: { "content-type": "application/x-www-form-urlencoded" },
    body,
  });
  if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`Google 인증 실패 (${response.status})`);
  const payload = await response.json();
  if (!payload.access_token) throw new Error("Google 인증 토큰을 받지 못했습니다.");
  return payload.access_token;
}

async function readSheetBatch(token, spreadsheetId, ranges, attempt = 0) {
  if(String(token||'').startsWith('UEP_SCHOOL_READ:')) return schoolReadBatchRead(spreadsheetId,ranges);

  url.searchParams.set("valueRenderOption", "UNFORMATTED_VALUE");
  url.searchParams.set("dateTimeRenderOption", "SERIAL_NUMBER");
  ranges.forEach((range) => url.searchParams.append("ranges", range));
  const response = await fetch(url, { headers: { authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } });
  if (!response.ok) {
    let detail = "";
    try { detail = (await response.json())?.error?.message || ""; } catch {}
    if ((response.status === 429 || response.status >= 500) && attempt < 3) {
      const retryAfter = Number(response.headers.get("retry-after") || 0);
      const waitMs = retryAfter > 0 ? retryAfter * 1000 : 900 * Math.pow(2, attempt);

async function readSheetBatch(token, spreadsheetId, ranges, attempt = 0) {
  if(String(token||'').startsWith('UEP_SCHOOL_READ:')) return schoolReadBatchRead(spreadsheetId,ranges);
  const url = new URL(`https://sheets.googleapis.com/v4/spreadsheets/${spreadsheetId}/values:batchGet`);
  url.searchParams.set("majorDimension", "ROWS");
  url.searchParams.set("valueRenderOption", "UNFORMATTED_VALUE");
  url.searchParams.set("dateTimeRenderOption", "SERIAL_NUMBER");
  ranges.forEach((range) => url.searchParams.append("ranges", range));
  const response = await fetch(url, { headers: { authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } });
  if (!response.ok) {
    let detail = "";
    try { detail = (await response.json())?.error?.message || ""; } catch {}
    if ((response.status === 429 || response.status >= 500) && attempt < 3) {
      const retryAfter = Number(response.headers.get("retry-after") || 0);
      const waitMs = retryAfter > 0 ? retryAfter * 1000 : 900 * Math.pow(2, attempt);
      await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, waitMs));
      return readSheetBatch(token, spreadsheetId, ranges, attempt + 1);
    }
    throw new Error(`UEP 구글시트 읽기 실패 (${response.status})${detail ? `: ${detail}` : ""}`);
  }
  return (await response.json()).valueRanges || [];
}

function sheetNameFromA1Range(range="") {
  const prefix=String(range||"").split("!")[0].trim();
  return prefix.replace(/^'/,"").replace(/'$/,"").replace(/''/g,"'");
}

function assignValueRangesByRange(target, entries, valueRanges) {
  const logicalBySheet=new Map(entries.map(([logicalName,range])=>[sheetNameFromA1Range(range),logicalName]));
  const assigned=new Set();
  (Array.isArray(valueRanges)?valueRanges:[]).forEach((item,index)=>{
    const responseSheet=sheetNameFromA1Range(item?.range);
    // Sheets batchGet may omit empty ranges or return them in a different order.
    // A response that names its range must only be assigned to that sheet; index is
    // retained solely for legacy responses that contain 

===== electron\main.cjs :: spreadsheets @ 41102 =====
));
  const assigned=new Set();
  (Array.isArray(valueRanges)?valueRanges:[]).forEach((item,index)=>{
    const responseSheet=sheetNameFromA1Range(item?.range);
    // Sheets batchGet may omit empty ranges or return them in a different order.
    // A response that names its range must only be assigned to that sheet; index is
    // retained solely for legacy responses that contain no range metadata at all.
    const logicalName=responseSheet?logicalBySheet.get(responseSheet):entries[index]?.[0];
    if(!logicalName)return;
    target[logicalName]=item?.values||[];
    assigned.add(logicalName);
  });
  entries.forEach(([logicalName])=>{if(!assigned.has(logicalName)&&!target[logicalName])target[logicalName]=[];});
  return target;
}

async function updateSheetValues(token, spreadsheetId, range, values, attempt=0) {
  const url = new URL(`https://sheets.googleapis.com/v4/spreadsheets/${spreadsheetId}/values/${encodeURIComponent(range)}`);
  url.searchParams.set("valueInputOption", "USER_ENTERED");
  const response = await fetch(url, {
    method: "PUT",
    headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`, "Content-Type": "application/json" },
    body: JSON.stringify({ majorDimension: "ROWS", values }),
  });
  if (!response.ok) {
    let detail=""; try{ detail=(await response.json())?.error?.message||""; }catch{}
    if ((response.status===429 || response.status>=500) && attempt<4) {
      const retryAfter=Number(response.headers.get("retry-after")||0);

async function batchUpdateSheetValues(token, spreadsheetId, data, attempt=0) {
  const items=(Array.isArray(data)?data:[]).filter(x=>x&&x.range&&Array.isArray(x.values));
  if(!items.length)return {totalUpdatedRows:0};
  const url=new URL(`https://sheets.googleapis.com/v4/spreadsheets/${spreadsheetId}/values:batchUpdate`);
  const response=await fetch(url,{
    method:"POST",
    headers:{Authorization:`Bearer ${token}`,"Content-Type":"application/json"},
    body:JSON.stringify({valueInputOption:"USER_ENTERED",data:items.map(x=>({range:x.range,majorDimension:"ROWS",values:x.values}))}),
  });
  if(!response.ok){
    let detail="";try{detail=(await response.json())?.error?.message||"";}catch{}
    if((response.status===429||response.status>=500)&&attempt<4){
      const retryAfter=Number(response.headers.get("retry-after")||0);

      await new Promise(resolve=>setTimeout(resolve,waitMs));
      return batchUpdateSheetValues(token,spreadsheetId,items,attempt+1);

    throw new Error(`구글시트 일괄 저장 실패 (${response.status})${detail?`: ${detail}`:""}`);
  }
  return response.json();
}


function columnLetter(index) {
  let n=Number(index)+1, out="";
  while(n>0){ const r=(n-1)%26; out=String.fromCharCode(65+r)+out; n=Math.floor((n-1)/26); }
  return out;
}
function normalizeSheetHeader(value){return String(value??"").replace(/\r?\n/g," ").replace(/\s+/g," ").trim();}
function findHeaderRowByRequired(matrix,required){
  const wanted=required.map(x=>String(x));
  const limit=Math.min(Array.isArray(matrix)?matrix.length:0,12);
  for(let i=0;i<limit;i+=1){
    const row=(matrix[i]||[]).map(normalizeSheetHeader);
    if(wanted.every(name=>row.includes(name))) return {index:i,headers:row};
  }
  return null;
}

function officialAttendanceRow(payload={}, id="") {
  const now=new Date().toISOString();
  const type=String(payload.detailType||"결석");
  const start=String(payload.startPeriod||"");
  const end=String(payload.endPeriod||"");
  const unit=(start||end)?"교시":"일일";
  const scope=(start||end)?(start&&end?`${start}~${end}교시`:`${start||end}교시`):"하루 전체";
  return [[
    id,
    String(payload.studentId||""), String(payload.studentNo||""), String(payload.name||""),
    String(payload.grade||"1"), String(payload.className||payload.classNo||""), String(payload.date||""),
    String(payload.attendanceType||"출석인정"), type, start, end,
    String(payload.reasonText||payload.reason||""), String(payload.place||""),
    String(payload.evidenceRequired||"N"), String(payload.evidence||"불필요"),
    String(payload.signatureRequired||"N"), String(payload.signatureStatus||"불필요"),
    String(payload.registrar||"UEP 직접등록"), now, String(payload.editor||"UEP"), String(payload.note||""),
    unit, scope, String(payload.originalType||"공결"), String(payload.sourceTab||"UEP 직접등록"), String(payload.sourceRow||"")
  ]];
}

function sheetNameFromA1Range(range="") {
  const prefix=String(range||"").split("!")[0].trim();
  return prefix.replace(/^'/,"").replace(/'$/,"").replace(/''/g,"'");
}

function assignValueRangesByRange(target, entries, valueRanges) {
  const logicalBySheet=new Map(entries.map(([logicalName,range])=>[sheetNameFromA1Range(range),logicalName]));
  const assigned=new Set();
  (Array.isArray(valueRanges)?valueRanges:[]).forEach((item,index)=>{
    const responseSheet=sheetNameFromA1Range(item?.range);
    // Sheets batchGet may omit empty ranges or return them in a different order.
    // A response that names its range must only be assigned to that sheet; index is
    // retained solely for legacy responses that contain no range metadata at all.
    const logicalName=responseSheet?logicalBySheet.get(responseSheet):entries[index]?.[0];
    if(!logicalName)return;
    target[logicalName]=item?.values||[];
    assigned.add(logicalName);
  });
  entries.forEach(([logicalName])=>{if(!assigned.has(logicalName)&&!target[logicalName])target[logicalName]=[];});
  return target;
}

function ymd(value = new Date()) {
  const date = value instanceof Date ? value : new Date(value);
  return `${date.getFullYear()}${String(date.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, "0")}${String(date.getDate()).padStart(2, "0")}`;
}

function mondayOf(dateValue) {
  const date = dateValue instanceof Date ? new Date(dateValue) : new Date(dateValue || Date.now());
  date.setHours(12, 0, 0, 0);
  const day = date.getDay();
  date.setDate(date.getDate() - (day === 0 ? 6 : day - 1));
  return date;
}

async function fetchJson(url, timeoutMs = 12000) {
  const controller = new AbortController();
  const timer = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), timeoutMs);
  try {
    const response = await fetch(url, {
      signal: controller.signal,
      headers: { "user-agent": "UEP/0.42.7 School Operations" },
    });
    if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`공공데이터 연결 실패 (${response.status})`);
    return await response.json();
  } finally {
    clearTimeout(timer);
  }
}

function neisRows(payload, key) {
  const group = payload?.[key];
  if (!Array.isArray(group)) return [];
  const rowBlock = group.find((item) => Array.isArray(item?.row));
  return rowBlock?.row || [];
}

function neisResultMessage(payload, key) {
  const result = payload?.RESULT || payload?.[key]?.[0]?.head?.find?.((item) => item.RESULT)?.RESULT;
  if (!result) return "";
  return result.MESSAGE || result.CODE || "";
}

function dashboardAdmissionMethod(row){
  const text=[row['선발방식'],row['평가구조요약'],row['핵심평가'],row['주요평가자료'],row['한줄요약'],row['상담표시문구']].filter(Boolean).join(' ');
  if(row['선발방식'])return String(row['선발방식']);if(row['평가구조요약'])return String(row['평가구조요약']);
  const out=[];if(/교과|내신/.test(text))out.push('내신');if(/수능최저|최저/.test(text))out.push('수능최저');if(/서류|학생부/.test(text))out.push('서류');if(/면접/.test(text))out.push('면접');if(/논술/.test(text))out.unshift('논술');if(/수능/.test(text)&&/정시|수능/.test(String(row['전형유형']||'')))return '수능100 또는 영역별 반영';return [...new Set(out)].join(' + ')||'대학별 방식 확인';
}

function uep08249MajorResults(query,region,type,minimum){
  const q=String(query||'').trim().toLowerCase();
  const mins=dashboardAdmissionRows('admissionMinimumRows','54_수능최저DB').filter(r=>uep08258AdmissionActive(r));
  const structures=dashboardAdmissionStructureRows().filter(r=>uep08258AdmissionActive(r));
  const unis=dashboardAdmissionUniversities();
  const uniByNorm=new Map(unis.map(u=>[dashboardAdmissionNormalizeUniversity(u['대학명']),u]));
  const out=[];
  for(const m of mins){const text=uep08249MajorText(m);if(q&&!text.toLowerCase().includes(q))continue;const norm=dashboardAdmissionNormalizeUniversity(m['대학명']);const u=uniByNorm.get(norm)||{'대학명':m['대학명'],'캠퍼스':m['캠퍼스']};if(region&&typeof uep08223UniversityRegions==='function'&&!uep08223UniversityRegions(u).includes(region))continue;const track=String(m['전형명']||m['전형유형']||'').trim();const linked=structures.filter(s=>dashboardAdmissionNormalizeUniversity(s['대학명'])===norm&&(!track||String(s['전형명']||'').includes(track)||track.includes(String(s['전형명']||''))));const s=linked[0]||structures.find(x=>dashboardAdmissionNormalizeUniversity(x['대학명'])===norm)||{};const broad=String(s['대전형']||s['전형유형']||m['전형유형']||'').trim();if(type&&broad!==type)continue;const minText=String(m['수능최저원문']||m['수능최저']||m['최저기준']||'').trim();if(minimum==='none'&&!/미적용|없음|해당없음/.test(minText))continue;if(minimum==='yes'&&/미적용|없음|해당없음/.test(minText))continue;out.push({u,m,s,broad,track,minText,text})}
  const seen=new Set();return out.filter(x=>{const k=[x.u['대학명'],x.track,x.text,x.minText].join('|');if(seen.has(k))return false;seen.add(k);return true}).slice(0,120)
}

function dashboardAdmissionMethod(row){
  const text=[row['선발방식'],row['평가구조요약'],row['핵심평가'],row['주요평가자료'],row['한줄요약'],row['상담표시문구']].filter(Boolean).join(' ');
  if(row['선발방식'])return String(row['선발방식']);if(row['평가구조요약'])return String(row['평가구조요약']);
  const out=[];if(/교과|내신/.test(text))out.push('내신');if(/수능최저|최저/.test(text))out.push('수능최저');if(/서류|학생부/.test(text))out.push('서류');if(/면접/.test(text))out.push('면접');if(/논술/.test(text))out.unshift('논술');if(/수능/.test(text)&&/정시|수능/.test(String(row['전형유형']||'')))return '수능100 또는 영역별 반영';return [...new Set(out)].join(' + ')||'대학별 방식 확인';
}

function dashboardAdmissionMethod(row){
  const text=[row['선발방식'],row['평가구조요약'],row['핵심평가'],row['주요평가자료'],row['한줄요약'],row['상담표시문구']].filter(Boolean).join(' ');
  if(row['선발방식'])return String(row['선발방식']);if(row['평가구조요약'])return String(row['평가구조요약']);
  const out=[];if(/교과|내신/.test(text))out.push('내신');if(/수능최저|최저/.test(text))out.push('수능최저');if(/서류|학생부/.test(text))out.push('서류');if(/면접/.test(text))out.push('면접');if(/논술/.test(text))out.unshift('논술');if(/수능/.test(text)&&/정시|수능/.test(String(row['전형유형']||'')))return '수능100 또는 영역별 반영';return [...new Set(out)].join(' + ')||'대학별 방식 확인';
}

async function loadWeather() {
  try {
    const response = await fetch(
      "https://api.open-meteo.com/v1/forecast?latitude=36.6424&longitude=127.4890&current=temperature_2m,apparent_temperature,relative_humidity_2m,weather_code,precipitation,wind_speed_10m&hourly=temperature_2m,relative_humidity_2m,precipitation_probability,weather_code,wind_speed_10m&daily=weather_code,temperature_2m_max,temperature_2m_min,precipitation_probability_max&timezone=Asia%2FSeoul&forecast_days=8",
    );
    if (!response.ok) throw new Error("weather unavailable");
    const data = await response.json();
    const now = new Date();
    const currentHour = now.getHours();

    const currentIndex = Math.max(0, data.hourly.time.indexOf(hourKey));

      code: data.current.weather_code,
      humidity: data.current.relative_humidity_2m ?? 0,
      wind: Math.round((data.current.wind_speed_10m ?? 0) * 10) / 10,
      rain: data.hourly.precipitation_probability[currentIndex] ?? 0,
      precipitation: data.current.precipitation ?? 0,
      daily: data.daily.time.map((time, index) => ({
        time,
        code: data.daily.weather_code[index],
        max: Math.round(data.daily.temperature_2m_max[index]),
        min: Math.round(data.daily.temperature_2m_min[index]),
        rain: data.daily.precipitation_probability_max[index] ?? 0,
      })),
      hourly: data.hourly.time.slice(currentIndex, currentIndex + 8).map((time, offset) => {
        const index = currentIndex + offset;
        return {
          time,
          code: data.hourly.weather_code[i

function uepSharedSettingValue08166(key){
  const rows=Array.isArray(readonlyCache?.settings)?readonlyCache.settings:[];
  const hit=rows.find(r=>String(r?.key||r?.settingKey||r?.['설정키']||'').trim()===String(key));
  return String(hit?.value||hit?.settingValue||hit?.['설정값']||'').trim();
}
function uepUpsertLocalSharedSetting08166(key,value,description=''){
  if(!readonlyCache)readonlyCache={};
  if(!Array.isArray(readonlyCache.settings))readonlyCache.settings=[];
  const rows=readonlyCache.settings;
  const hit=rows.find(r=>String(r?.key||r?.settingKey||r?.['설정키']||'').trim()===String(key));
  const entry={key:String(key),value:String(value),description:String(description),settingKey:String(key),settingValue:String(value),'설정키':String(key),'설정값':String(value),'설명':String(description)};
  if(hit)Object.assign(hit,entry);else rows.push(entry);
}
async function uepSaveSharedSecurity08166(key,value,description){
  if(!window.schoolBoard?.saveSharedSecurityConfig)throw new Error('공용 보안설정 저장 기능을 사용할 수 없습니다.');
  const result=await window.schoolBoard.saveSharedSecurityConfig({key,value,description,editor:currentLoginTeacherName?.()||''});
  if(!result?.ok)throw new Error(result?.reason||'공용 보안설정을 저장하지 못했습니다.');
  uepUpsertLocalSharedSetting08166(key,value,description);
  return true;
}

}

function parseStudentLine(line) {
  const trimmed = String(line || "").trim();
  if (!trimmed) return { number: "", name: "" };
  const parts = trimmed.includes("\t")
    ? trimmed.split(/\t+/)
    : trimmed.includes(",")
      ? trimmed.split(/,+/)
      : trimmed.split(/\s+/);
  return { number: (parts.shift() || "").trim(), name: parts.join(" ").trim() };
}

function openProgramSheetSettings() {
  $("#drawerKicker").textContent = "GOOGLE SHEETS";
  $("#drawerTitle").textContent = "프로그램 시트 연결 준비";
  $("#drawerBody").innerHTML =
    `<form class="site-editor" id="programSheetForm"><label>구글시트 주소<input id="programSheetUrl" type="url" value="${escapeHtml(state.settings.programSheetUrl || "")}" placeholder="https://docs.google.com/spreadsheets/..."></label><label>프로그램 탭 이름<input id="programSheetName" value="${escapeHtml(state.settings.programSheetName || "프로그램")}"></label><div class="sheet-schema"><b>권장 열 구조</b><span>운영일 · 프로그램명 · 시작시간 · 종료시간 · 장소 · 담당교사 · 학번 · 이름 · 안내</span></div><button class="btn primary">연결 정보 저장</button><p class="safe-note">여기서는 주소와 탭 이름만 저장합니다. 실제 읽기·쓰기는 권한 확인과 선생님 승인 후 연결합니다.</p></form>`;
  $("#drawer").classList.remove("hidden");
  $("#drawerBackdrop").classList.remove("hidden");
  $("#programSheetForm").onsubmit = async (event) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    state.settings.programSheetUrl = $("#programSheetUrl").value.trim();
    state.settings.programSheetName = $("#programSheetName").value.trim() || "프로그램";
    await save();
    toast("시트 연결 정보를 저장했습니다.");
    closeDrawer();
  };
}


function upsertNoticeCache(payload,id){
  readonlyCache=readonlyCache||{};readonlyCache.notices=Array.isArray(readonlyCache.notices)?readonlyCache.notices:[];
  const normalized={...payload,id:String(id||payload.id||''),status:payload.status||'게시',detail:payload.content,date:payload.postDate,receipts:readonlyCache.notices.find(x=>String(x.id)===String(id))?.receipts||[]};
  const index=readonlyCache.notices.findIndex(x=>String(x.id)===String(normalized.id));
  if(index>=0)readonlyCache.notices[index]={...readonlyCache.notices[index],...normalized};else readonlyCache.notices.unshift(normalized);
  if(Array.isArray(state.workNotices)&&state.workNotices.length){state.workNotices=[];save().catch(()=>{});}
  return normalized;
}

function inputCenterExpandTextFile(file){
  const raw=String(file?.text||'').replace(/\r/g,'');
  const structured=inputCenterSpreadsheetRows(file,raw);
  const classification=inputCenterClassifyMaterial(file,raw);
  if(structured){structured.forEach(d=>{const q=(d.sheetHeaders||[]).filter(h=>/^\s*\d+[.)]/.test(String(h||''))).length;const isReport=q>=3||/보고서|제출내역_텍스트/.test(String(d.sheetName||''));d.detectedType=isReport?'보고서·탐구보고서':classification.type;d.detectedConfidence=isReport?'높음':classification.confidence;d.detectedReason=isReport?`Excel 문항형 보고서 · ${q}개 문항 직접 인식`:classification.reason;});return structured;}
  if(!raw.trim()){const d=inputCenterDraftFromPayload(file,'');d.detectedType=classification.type;d.detectedConfidence=classification.confidence;d.detectedReason=classification.reason;return [d];}
  const lines=raw.split('\n').filter(x=>x.trim());
  const studentLines=lines.filter(line=>/(?:^|[^0-9])[123][0-9]{3}(?![0-9])/.test(line));
  const drafts=(studentLines.length>=2 && studentLines.length>=Math.min(3,Math.ceil(lines.length*.25)))?studentLines.map(line=>inputCenterDraftFromPayload({...file,name:file.name},line)):[inputCenterDraftFromPayload(file,raw)];
  drafts.forEach(d=>{d.detectedType=classification.type;d.detectedConfidence=classification.confidence;d.detectedReason=classification.reason;});return drafts;
}

function openConnectionDetail(key) {
  const item = state.settings.dataConnections?.[key];
  if (!item) return;
  const columns = key === "studentMaster"
    ? "학급 · 번호 · 성명 · 출신학교 · 입학자료 · 학생/보호자 연락처 · 생년월일 · 비고"
    : "학급 · 번호 · 성명 · 학사 방 · 퇴사 · 입사 · 학기별 변동";
  $("#drawerKicker").textContent = "READ ONLY SOURCE";
  $("#drawerTitle").textContent = item.label;

  $("#drawer").classList.remove("hidden");
  $("#drawerBackdrop").classList.remove("hidden");
}

function openSchoolCalendarEvent(id) {
  const event = allSchoolEvents().find((item) => item.id === id);
  if (!event) return;
  const date = new Date(`${event.date}T00:00:00`);
  $("#drawerKicker").textContent = "SCHOOL CALENDAR";
  $("#drawerTitle").textContent = event.title;
  $("#drawerBody").innerHTML = `<div class="calendar-event-fields"><div><b>대상</b><span>${escapeHtml(event.target || "원문 확인")}</span></div><div><b>시간</b><span>${escapeHtml(event.time || "종일·미정")}</span></div><div><b>장소</b><span>${escapeHtml(event.place || "원문 확인")}</span></div><div><b>프로그램명</b><span>${escapeHtml(event.title)}</span></div><div><b>부서·담당자</b><span>${escapeHtml(event.owner || "원문 확인")}</span></div></div><div class="connection-detail"><div><b>날짜</b><span>${date.getFullYear()}년 ${date.getMonth() + 1}월 ${date.getDate()}일</span></div><div><b>구분</b><span>${escapeHtml(event.type)}</span></div><div><b>상태</b><span>${escapeHtml(event.status || "상태 미기재")}</span></div><div><b>출처</b><span>${escapeHtml(event.source)}</span></div></div><div class="readonly-rule"><b>일정 원문</b><span>${escapeHtml(event.detail)}</span></div><div class="drawer-actions calendar-detail-actions"><button class="btn primary" data-calendar-edit="${escapeHtml(event.id)}">⚙ 일정 수정·보완</button></div><p class="drawer-note">수정 내용은 41_학교캘린더의 UEP 보정값으로 저장되며, 월간·주간·오늘의 프로그램에 공통 반영됩니다.

function writeDaily_(rows) {

    dateColumns: [3, 20, 21, 23, 30],
    timeColumns: [12, 13, 14],
  });

function writeSnapshot_(sheetName, rows, width, formats) {
  const sheet = SpreadsheetApp.getActive().getSheetByName(sheetName);
  const oldRows = Math.max(0, sheet.getLastRow() - 2);
  const clearRows = Math.max(oldRows, rows.length);
  if (clearRows > 0) sheet.getRange(3, 1, clearRows, width).clearContent();

  if (!rows.length) return;
  sheet.getRange(3, 1, rows.length, width).setValues(rows);

  (formats.dateColumns || []).forEach((col) => {
    sheet.getRange(3, col, rows.length, 1).setNumberFormat('yyyy-mm-dd hh:mm');
  });
  (formats.timeColumns || []).forEach((col) => {
    sheet.getRange(3, col, rows.length, 1).setNumberFormat('@');
  });
}

function writeErrors_(rows) {
  const sheet = SpreadsheetApp.getActive().getSheetByName(UEP_DORM_PROCESSOR.ERROR_SHEET);
  const width = 10;
  const oldRows = Math.max(0, sheet.getLastRow() - 2);
  const clearRows = Math.max(oldRows, rows.length);
  if (clearRows > 0) sheet.getRange(3, 1, clearRows, width).clearContent();
  if (rows.length) {
    sheet.getRange(3, 1, rows.length, width).setValues(rows);
    sheet.getRange(3, 1, rows.length, 1).setNumberFormat('yyyy-mm-dd hh:mm');
  }
}

function markDailyAsSent_() {
  const sheet = SpreadsheetApp.getActive().getSheetByName(UEP_DORM_PROCESSOR.DAILY_SHEET);
  const count = Math.max(0, sheet.getLastRow() - 2);
  if (!count) return;
  const now = new Date();
  sheet.getRange(3, 22, count, 1).setValue('전송완료');
  sheet.getRange(3, 23, count, 1).setValue(now).setNumberFormat('yyyy-mm-dd hh:mm');
}

function readNormalizedRows_() {
  const sheet = SpreadsheetApp.getActive().getSheetByName(UEP_DORM_PROCESSOR.NORMALIZED_SHEET);
  if (sheet.getLastRow() < 3) return [];
  return sheet.getRange(3, 1, sheet.getLastRow() - 2, 25).getValues()
    .filter((r) => r[0]);
}
